A task chooses one of multiple identical objects by a spatial relation, and wrist views can contain the wrong duplicate.\\n- [Select identical targets by the named spatial relation before wrist refinement](global/relation-selected-identical-object.md) \\u2014 A task contains duplicate or visually identical objects and names a relation such as on, left of, right of, between, or next to\\n- placement depends on body-to-EEF offset\\n- [After releasing into a lined basket, retreat straight up before moving laterally](global/retreat-high-before-lateral-near-lined-basket.md) \\u2014 placing central visual bands fail despite clean object placement\\n- [Use a capped contact skill to seat rim-perched objects into containers.](global/rim-perch-contact-seat.md) \\u2014 An object has been released at a rimmed container and is visibly perched on the lip or liner while the In predicate is still false.\\n- [A large rotate_wrist yaw drifts the eef ~0.1-0.15m; re-center before look-alike cans by RGB label, never by SAM3 brand-noun prompt](global/sam3-brand-noun-can-collision.md) \\u2014 two or more similar cans/cylinders are present and the task names one by brand (\\\"tomato sauce\\\", \\\"alphabet soup\\\", a soup can)\\n- [Re-issue a pick with a nearby landmark when generic picking makes only contact.](global/spatial-qualified-repick.md) \\u2014 A generic
